  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "si-s1",
      "url": "https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E5%85%95",
      "title": "兕",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia",
      "published": null,
      "language": "ja",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-01"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "si-c01",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "兕（呉音: じ、漢音: し、拼音: sì）は、古代中国の文献に出てくる動物の一種。牛に似た一本角の獣。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 1",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Si adalah hewan bertanduk satu yang menyerupai sapi dalam teks kuno Tiongkok; identifikasi zoologisnya memerlukan kehati-hatian.",
        "en": "Si is a single horned oxlike animal in ancient Chinese texts; its zoological identification requires care."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "si-c02",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "『論語』や『荘子』といった先秦の様々な文献で、「虎」や「蛟」（蛟竜）と並ぶ猛獣の代名詞として言及される。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 2",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Teks seperti Analects dan Zhuangzi menyebut Si sebagai contoh hewan buas bersama harimau dan jiao.",
        "en": "Texts such as the Analects and Zhuangzi mention Si as a fierce beast alongside tigers and jiao."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "si-c03",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "『爾雅』郭璞注などによれば、一本角で、色は青（青黒）、重さは千斤だった。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 3",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Komentar Guo Pu pada Erya menggambarkannya bertanduk satu dan berwarna biru gelap.",
        "en": "Guo Pu's Erya commentary describes it as single horned and dark blue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "si-c04",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "『山海経』によれば、兕は「犀」などと一緒に中国内外各地に生息していた。『爾雅』本文によれば、兕が牛に似ているのに対し、犀は豚に似ていた。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 4",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Erya membandingkan Si dengan sapi dan xi dengan babi.",
        "en": "The Erya compares Si with an ox and xi with a pig."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "si-c05",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "兕は楚国と縁が深い。『古本竹書紀年』によれば、周の昭王が楚に南征に訪れた際、大兕に遭遇したという。また様々な文献において、楚の君主が雲夢の地で御狩する猛獣として登場する（例:『公孫龍子』跡府篇などに伝わる「楚弓楚得」説話、『戦国策』楚策一、『楚辞』招魂）。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 5",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejumlah teks menghubungkan Si dengan perburuan penguasa Chu.",
        "en": "Several texts associate Si with the hunts of Chu rulers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "si-c06",
      "source_id": "si-s1",
      "quote": "『周礼』考工記などによれば、兕や犀の皮革は甲冑の材料として利用されていた。『荀子』議兵篇などによれば、そのような用法は楚人の慣習だった。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 6",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zhou Li dan teks lain menyebut kulit Si sebagai bahan untuk baju zirah.",
        "en": "The Zhou Li and other texts mention Si hides as material for armour."
      }
    }
  ],

Adiknya membunuh rusa itu, lalu kembali dan menemukan raksasa lelaki yang membunuhnya. Versi lain mengisahkan kulit rusa yang terbang mencekik pemburu setelah larangan mengenai empedunya dilanggar.",

      "id": "the-great-deer-c04",
      "source_id": "the-great-deer-s1",
      "quote": "傳說中以前有對兄弟長期輪流去宿瓦納（Nsoana，位於今嘉義縣阿里山鄉）的山上獵鹿，鹿群中有頭鹿只要被武器瞄準就會越變越大，哥哥知道那頭鹿不可隨意射殺，所以都會改獵別隻，並且告訴弟弟，但不服輸的弟弟在一次打獵中發現那頭鹿，並將牠射殺，又因為太緊張了，直接丟下鹿王跑回家，後來他折返回去查看時（他有請哥哥跟他一起去，但半路上哥哥就跑回家了），沒有找到鹿，但草叢中卻出現一個眼睛如日月般明亮的男巨人，男巨人怒吼著抓住弟弟，用力甩了幾下後把他摔向旁邊的樹幹，弟弟就此斃命。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 3",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita dua bersaudara pemburu, sang kakak menghindari rusa yang membesar ketika dibidik.",
        "en": "In a story of two hunting brothers, the elder avoids a deer that grows larger when aimed at."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "the-great-deer-c05",
      "source_id": "the-great-deer-s1",
      "quote": "後來他折返回去查看時（他有請哥哥跟他一起去，但半路上哥哥就跑回家了），沒有找到鹿，但草叢中卻出現一個眼睛如日月般明亮的男巨人，男巨人怒吼著抓住弟弟，用力甩了幾下後把他摔向旁邊的樹幹，弟弟就此斃命。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 3",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Adiknya membunuh rusa itu, lalu kembali dan menemukan raksasa lelaki yang membunuhnya.",
        "en": "The younger brother kills the deer, then returns and encounters a male giant who kills him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "the-great-deer-c06",
      "source_id": "the-great-deer-s1",
      "quote": "除了以上傳說外，也有鹿變成人負傷而逃，並且趁機把追上來的獵人的頭抓去撞樹幹來殺死他、或是鹿在死前告訴獵人不要刺破牠的膽，但獵人還是刺破了，導致剝下來的鹿皮飛去勒死了獵人的版本。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 4",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Versi lain mengisahkan kulit rusa yang terbang mencekik pemburu setelah larangan mengenai empedunya dilanggar.",
        "en": "Another version describes a deer hide flying to strangle a hunter after a warning about its gall bladder is violated."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Water Monkey adalah makhluk air dalam cerita rakyat Tiongkok yang juga disebut water ghost. Habitat yang dikisahkan berupa perairan tawar; makanannya terutama ikan dan udang.",
      "en": "The Water Monkey is an aquatic creature in Chinese folklore also called a water ghost. Its reported habitat is fresh water, and its food is mainly fish and shrimp.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "water-monkey-c01",
        "water-monkey-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Versi cerita berbeda antara hewan mirip kera dan arwah orang yang tenggelam. Ia dikatakan menarik manusia atau hewan dari tepi air ke dalam air. Dalam air kekuatannya digambarkan sangat besar. Kekuatan gaibnya dikatakan hilang ketika berada di daratan.",
      "en": "Versions differ between a monkeylike animal and the ghost of a drowned person. It is said to drag humans or animals from the shore into the water. In the water it is described as immensely strong. Its supernatural strength is said to disappear on land.",

        "water-monkey-c03",
        "water-monkey-c04",
        "water-monkey-c05",

      ]

  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
  "story_mode": null,

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c01",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "水猴子，又称水猴、水鬼、水狮鬼等，是一种中国民间传说中的神秘生物。据说它生存于淡水区域，具有两栖的特征；主要以水中的鱼虾为食，偶尔害人淹死后也会吸尸体的血。 对于水猴子的真面目，有水獭一说。传说还常将水猴子和河童混为一谈。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 1",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Water Monkey adalah makhluk air dalam cerita rakyat Tiongkok yang juga disebut water ghost.",
        "en": "The Water Monkey is an aquatic creature in Chinese folklore also called a water ghost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c02",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "水猴子，又称水猴、水鬼、水狮鬼等，是一种中国民间传说中的神秘生物。据说它生存于淡水区域，具有两栖的特征；主要以水中的鱼虾为食，偶尔害人淹死后也会吸尸体的血。 对于水猴子的真面目，有水獭一说。传说还常将水猴子和河童混为一谈。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 1",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Habitat yang dikisahkan berupa perairan tawar; makanannya terutama ikan dan udang.",
        "en": "Its reported habitat is fresh water, and its food is mainly fish and shrimp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c03",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "有说法认为水猴子是一种类似于猴子的未知动物。也有说法认为水猴子是溺水死亡的人的鬼魂。两种说法的共同之处都是水猴子都会攻击杀害在水中的人。",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 2",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Versi cerita berbeda antara hewan mirip kera dan arwah orang yang tenggelam.",
        "en": "Versions differ between a monkeylike animal and the ghost of a drowned person."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c04",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "（1）水猴子会把岸上的人或动物拖下水； （2）水猴子在水里力大无穷，一旦被其拖入水中便很难上岸；",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 4",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dikatakan menarik manusia atau hewan dari tepi air ke dalam air.",
        "en": "It is said to drag humans or animals from the shore into the water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c05",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "（2）水猴子在水里力大无穷，一旦被其拖入水中便很难上岸； （3",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 5",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam air kekuatannya digambarkan sangat besar.",
        "en": "In the water it is described as immensely strong."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "water-monkey-c06",
      "source_id": "water-monkey-s1",
      "quote": "（4）一旦上岸便失去了神奇的力量； （5）水猴子惧怕红色。 显然这些特征是水獭说无法解释的，水獭只能袭击体型较小的生物",
      "locator": "Article text, paragraph 6",

      "statement": {
